the proofs, which were late again, and the foreman walked the length
of the room twice before he sat down.

Paragraph 748. Ink is patient. It waits in the tin for weeks and then,
on the one day that matters, it goes on thick and dark and even, and
the sheet comes off the platen looking as if it had always been
printed. Ink is patient. It waits in the tin for weeks and then, on
